\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathcal{E}_{i}(\widehat{C}_{n}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n}(\theta),\theta)+2\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)\|_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\textrm{LS}_{i}(\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)+\frac{2}{T}\int_{0}^{T}\Psi_{n,i}(t;\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)dM_{n,i}(t)+2\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)\|_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\textrm{LS}_{i}(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta),\theta)+\frac{2}{T}\int_{0}^{T}\Psi_{n,i}(t;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta),\theta)dM_{n,i}(t)+2\omega_{i}\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{T}\textrm{LS}_{i}(\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)+2\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\quad\quad-\frac{1}{T}\textrm{LS}_{i}(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta),\theta)-2\omega_{i}\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\frac{2}{T}\int_{0}^{T}\left[\Psi_{n,i}(t;\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)-\Psi_{n,i}(t;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta),\theta)\right]dM_{n,i}(t)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\mathcal{E}_{i}(C_{n}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n}^{*}(\theta),\theta)+2\omega_{i}\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\frac{2}{T}\int_{0}^{T}\left[\Psi_{n,i}(t;\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)-\Psi_{n,i}(t;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta),\theta)\right]dM_{n,i}(t),

where the last inequality uses the definition of
(\widehat{C}_{n}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n}(\theta))
. Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{2}{T}\int_{0}^{T}\left[\Psi_{n,i}(t;\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)-\Psi_{n,i}(t;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta),\theta)\right]dM_{n,i}(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{2}{T}\int_{0}^{T}\nu_{0}(X_{n,i}(t);\beta)dM_{n,i}(t)\left(\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta)\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\frac{2}{T}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{t-}g(t-r;\gamma)dN_{n,j}(r)dM_{n,i}(t)\left(\widehat{C}_{n,ij}(\theta)-C_{n,ij}^{*}(\theta)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{2}{T}\int_{0}^{T}\nu_{0}(X_{n,i}(t);\beta)dM_{n,i}(t)\right|\cdot\left|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta)\right|

\displaystyle+\sup_{j=1,...,n}\left|\frac{2}{T}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{0}^{t-}g(t-r;\gamma)dN_{n,j}(r)dM_{n,i}(t)\right|\cdot\left\|\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)-C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta)\right\|_{1}.

Both of the previous two displays together yield on
\mathcal{T}^{(i)}_{n}(a_{n},d_{n,i})
the following
basic inequality:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\mathcal{E}_{i}(\widehat{C}_{n}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n}(\theta),\theta)+2\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)\|_{1}\leq\frac{1}{T}\mathcal{E}_{i}(C_{n}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n}^{*}(\theta),\theta)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\omega_{i}\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta)\|_{1}+a_{n}\left|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta)\right|+d_{n,i}\left\|\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)-C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta)\right\|_{1}.

(71)

We apply (
71
) in the second inequality below. The first and fourth steps follow from
\omega_{i}\geq 3d_{n,i}
, and the last inequality is a consequence of the triangle inequality.

\displaystyle\frac{2}{T}\mathcal{E}_{i}(\widehat{C}_{n}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n}(\theta),\theta)+2\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}^{c}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{T}\mathcal{E}_{i}(\widehat{C}_{n}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n}(\theta),\theta)+4\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)\|_{1}-4\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\|_{1}-6d_{n,i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}^{c}(C^{*})}(\theta)\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{T}\mathcal{E}_{i}(C_{n}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n}^{*}(\theta),\theta)+4\omega_{i}\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta)\|_{1}-4\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\|_{1}-6d_{n,i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}^{c}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\|

\displaystyle+2a_{n}\left|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}\right|+2d_{n,i}\left\|\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)-C^{*}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{T}\mathcal{E}_{i}(C_{n}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n}^{*}(\theta),\theta)+4\omega_{i}\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta)\|_{1}-4\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\|_{1}-2d_{n,i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}^{c}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\|

\displaystyle+2a_{n}\left|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}\right|+2d_{n,i}\left\|\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)-C^{*}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{T}\mathcal{E}_{i}(C_{n}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n}^{*}(\theta),\theta)+4\omega_{i}\left(\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta)\|_{1}-\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\|_{1}\right)

\displaystyle+2a_{n}\left|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}\right|+\frac{2}{3}\omega_{i}\left\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)-C^{*}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{T}\mathcal{E}_{i}(C_{n}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n}^{*}(\theta),\theta)+\frac{14}{3}\omega_{i}\left\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C^{*}(\theta))}(\theta)-C^{*}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\right\|_{1}+2a_{n}\left|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}\right|.

By the definition of
\mathcal{E}_{i}(\cdot,\cdot,\cdot)
this is equivalent to

\displaystyle\frac{2}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}+2\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}^{c}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{14}{3}\omega_{i}\left\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C^{*}(\theta))}(\theta)-C^{*}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\right\|_{1}+2a_{n}\left|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}\right|.

(72)

We consider now two cases:

Case I
: Suppose that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\geq\frac{14}{3}\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)-C^{*}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\|_{1}+2a_{n}|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}(\theta)|.

(73)

In this case, (
72
) implies

\displaystyle\frac{2}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}+2\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}^{c}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}.

Using this, again, together with (
73
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{2}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}+2\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)-C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{6}{7}a_{n}\left|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta)\right|\leq\frac{24}{7}\cdot\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}

and, hence, after multiplication with
7/3

\displaystyle\frac{14}{3T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}+\frac{14}{3}\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)-C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2a_{n}\left|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta)\right|\leq 8\cdot\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2},

which is stronger than the inequality we want to prove.

Case II
: Suppose that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\frac{14}{3}\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)-C^{*}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\|_{1}+2a_{n}|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}(\theta)|.

In this case, we obtain from (
72
)

\displaystyle\frac{2}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}+2\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}^{c}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq 14\omega_{i}\left\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C^{*}(\theta))}(\theta)-C^{*}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\right\|_{1}+6a_{n}\left|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}\right|.

(74)

Since
L\geq\sup_{i=1,...,n}a_{n}/\omega_{i}
, this implies

\displaystyle\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}^{c}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\|_{1}\leq 7\left\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C^{*}(\theta))}(\theta)-C^{*}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\right\|_{1}+3L\left|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}\right|.

Hence,
(\widehat{C}_{n}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n}(\theta))\in\widetilde{{\cal R}}_{n}(i;\theta,L)
. This means by using the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality

\displaystyle 16\omega_{i}\left\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)-C^{*}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\right\|_{1}+8a_{n}\left|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 8\sqrt{4\omega_{i}^{2}|S_{i}(C_{n}^{*})|+a_{n}^{2}}\cdot\sqrt{\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}-C^{*}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\|_{2}^{2}+|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}(\theta)|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\sqrt{4\omega_{i}^{2}|S_{i}(C_{n}^{*})|+a_{n}^{2}}}{\phi_{i,\textrm{comp}}(L;\theta)}\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)-\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta),\theta)\right\|_{T}

\displaystyle\leq 18\cdot\frac{4\omega_{i}^{2}|S_{i}(C_{n}^{*})|+a_{n}^{2}}{\phi_{i,\textrm{comp}}^{2}(L;\theta)}+\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{8}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C^{*}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\alpha^{*}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2},

where we used in the last inequality that for any numbers
x,y,z\in\mathbb{R}

x(y+z)\leq\frac{9}{32}x^{2}+y^{2}+8z^{2}.

Thus, we obtain from (
74
) that

\displaystyle\frac{2}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}+2\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)-C^{*}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+2a_{n}|\widehat{\alpha}_{n}(\theta)_{n,i}-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}(\theta)|

\displaystyle\leq 14\omega_{i}\left\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)-C^{*}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\right\|_{1}+6a_{n}\left|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)-C^{*}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*}}(\theta)\|_{1}+2a_{n}|\widehat{\alpha}_{n}(\theta)_{n,i}-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}(\theta)|

\displaystyle=16\omega_{i}\left\|\widehat{C}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)-C^{*}_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}(\theta)\right\|_{1}+8a_{n}\left|\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta)-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 18\cdot\frac{4\omega_{i}^{2}|S_{i}(C_{n}^{*})|+a_{n}^{2}}{\phi_{i,\textrm{comp}}^{2}(L;\theta)}+\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{8}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C^{*}_{n,i}(\theta),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2},

which in turn implies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\widehat{\alpha}_{n,i}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}+2\omega_{i}\|\widehat{C}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)-C^{*}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta)\|_{1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+2a_{n}|\widehat{\alpha}_{n}(\theta)_{n,i}-\alpha^{*}_{n,i}(\theta)|

\displaystyle\leq 18\cdot\frac{4\omega_{i}^{2}|S_{i}(C_{n}^{*})|+a_{n}^{2}}{\phi_{i,\textrm{comp}}^{2}(L;\theta)}+\frac{8}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C^{*}_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}(\theta),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta),\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}.

∎

Proof of Lemma
C.4
.

We begin the proof with two observations. For the first observation, let

\displaystyle v_{i}(t;\theta):

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}\nu_{0}(X_{n,i}(t);\beta),&\int_{0}^{t-}g(t-r;\gamma)dN_{n,1}(r),&\cdots&\int_{0}^{t-}g(t-r;\gamma)dN_{n,n}(r)\end{pmatrix}^{T},

so that

\Psi_{n,i}(t;c,a,\theta)=v_{i}(t;\theta)^{T}\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix},\quad\lambda_{n,i}(t)=v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})^{T}\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
\big(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\big)^{T}\end{pmatrix}.

Using this, we can rewrite the criterion function from Lemma
3.7
as

\displaystyle\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;c,a,\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}\Bigg(v_{i}(t;\theta)^{T}\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}-v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})^{T}\binom{\alpha_{n,i}^{*}}{\big(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\big)^{T}}\Bigg)^{2}dt

\displaystyle=\binom{a}{c}^{T}\Big[\int_{0}^{T}v_{i}(t;\theta)v_{i}(t;\theta)^{T}dt\Big]\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}-2\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}^{T}\Big[\int_{0}^{T}v_{i}(t;\theta)v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})^{T}dt\Big]\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
\big(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\big)^{T}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\qquad+\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
\big(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\big)^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\Big[\int_{0}^{T}v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})^{T}dt\Big]\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
\big(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\big)^{T}\end{pmatrix}.

This, in turn, implies

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;c,a,\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}-\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;c,a,\theta_{n}^{*})-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}^{T}\Big[\frac{1}{T}\int_{0}^{T}v_{i}(t;\theta)v_{i}(t;\theta)^{T}dt\Big]\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}-2\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}^{T}\Big[\frac{1}{T}\int_{0}^{T}v_{i}(t;\theta)v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})^{T}dt\Big]\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
\big(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\big)^{T}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle+\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
\big(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\big)^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\Big[\frac{1}{T}\int_{0}^{T}v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})^{T}dt\Big]\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
\big(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\big)^{T}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle-\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}^{T}\Big[\frac{1}{T}\int_{0}^{T}v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})^{T}dt\Big]\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}+2\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}^{T}\Big[\frac{1}{T}\int_{0}^{T}v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})^{T}dt\Big]\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
\big(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\big)^{T}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle-\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
\big(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\big)^{T}\end{pmatrix}^{T}\Big[\frac{1}{T}\int_{0}^{T}v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})^{T}dt\Big]\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
\big(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\big)^{T}\end{pmatrix}\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}^{T}\Big[\frac{1}{T}\int_{0}^{T}\left(v_{i}(t;\theta)-v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})\right)\left(v_{i}(t;\theta)-v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})\right)^{T}dt\Big]\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle+2\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}^{T}\Big[\frac{1}{T}\int_{0}^{T}v_{i}(t;\theta)v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})^{T}dt\Big]\begin{pmatrix}a-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
c-\big(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\big)^{T}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle-\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}^{T}\Big[\frac{2}{T}\int_{0}^{T}v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})^{T}dt\Big]\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}+\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}^{T}\Big[\frac{2}{T}\int_{0}^{T}v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})^{T}dt\Big]\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
\big(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\big)^{T}\end{pmatrix}\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}^{T}\Big[\frac{1}{T}\int_{0}^{T}\left(v_{i}(t;\theta)-v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})\right)\left(v_{i}(t;\theta)-v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})\right)^{T}dt\Big]\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\begin{pmatrix}a\\
c\end{pmatrix}^{T}\Big[\frac{1}{T}\int_{0}^{T}\left(v_{i}(t;\theta)-v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})\right)v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})^{T}dt\Big]\begin{pmatrix}a-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
c-\big(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\big)^{T}\end{pmatrix}\Bigg|

Let
\mathcal{N}_{0}>0
be such that
\mathbb{P}(\Omega_{\mathcal{N}})\to 1
for
\mathcal{N}=6\mathcal{N}_{0}\log(nT)
according to Lemma
B.2
.
Note that, on
\Omega_{\mathcal{N}}
, we have that every entry of the difference
v_{i}(t;\theta)-v_{i}(t;\theta_{n}^{*})
can be uniformly (in
t
and
i
) be bounded by
\log(nT)\cdot\|\theta-\theta_{n}^{*}\|_{2}
times a constant.
Let us assume for the remainder of the proof that we are on the event
\Omega_{\mathcal{N}}
.
Let now
\theta\in\Theta
be arbitrary, and let
(c,a)
be such that
c
is
i
-th row of a matrix
C\in[0,\infty)^{n\times n}
, and
a
is the
i
-th entry of a vector
\alpha\in(0,\infty)^{n}
with
(C,\alpha)\in\mathcal{H}_{n}(\theta)\cup\mathcal{H}_{n}(\theta_{n}^{*})
and
c_{S_{i}(C_{n}^{*})^{c}}=0
.
In particular, then
a\in[0,K_{\alpha}]
.
For such
(a,c)
, we hence obtain on
\Omega_{\mathcal{N}}
for suitable constants
\mathcal{C}_{1},\mathcal{C}_{2}>0
that collect all previously mentioned constants

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;c,a,\theta)-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}-\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;c,a,\theta_{n}^{*})-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{1}(1+\|c\|_{1})^{2}\log^{2}(nT)\cdot\|\theta-\theta_{n}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathcal{C}_{2}(1+\|c\|_{1})\log^{2}(nT)\cdot\|\theta-\theta_{n}^{*}\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\begin{pmatrix}a-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
c-\left(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\right)^{T}\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{1}.

(75)

This was the first observation. For the second observation, recall that
C_{n}^{*}=C_{n}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*})
and
\alpha_{n}^{*}=\alpha_{n}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*})
. For any
a>0
and
c\in[0,\infty)^{n}
with
c_{S_{i}(C_{n}^{*})^{c}}=0
, we have that
(c,a)\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{n}(i;\theta_{n}^{*},L)
(recall the definition of the individual compatibility constant) and, hence,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;c,a,\theta_{n}^{*})-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;c,a,\theta_{n}^{*})-\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*}),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*}),\theta_{n}^{*})\right\|_{T}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\phi_{i,\textrm{comp}}(L;\theta_{n}^{*})^{2}\left(\left\|c_{S_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}-C_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*})\right\|_{2}^{2}+\left|a-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*})\right|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\phi_{i,\textrm{comp}}(L;\theta_{n}^{*})^{2}\frac{1}{1+|S_{i}(C_{n}^{*})|}\left\|\begin{pmatrix}a-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*})\\
c-C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*})\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{1}^{2}.

(76)

Now, using these two observations, we can make the following argument. Firstly, we note that (
75
) provides an upper bound on the value of the criterion function for
\overline{\theta}_{n}
at the minimizer because (
75
) shows that the criterion function for
\overline{\theta}_{n}
lies close to the criterion function for
\theta_{n}^{*}
. More precisely, by definition of
(\alpha_{n}^{*}(\theta),C_{n}^{*}(\theta))
and recalling that
\alpha_{n}^{*}=\alpha_{n}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*})
and
C_{n}^{*}=C_{n}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*})
, we obtain by (A2) that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n}),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n}),\overline{\theta}_{n})-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*}),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*}),\overline{\theta}_{n})-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*}),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*}),\theta_{n}^{*})-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}+\mathcal{C}_{1}(1+\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\|_{1})^{2}\log^{2}(nT)\cdot\left\|\overline{\theta}_{n}-\theta_{n}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{C}_{1}(1+\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\|_{1})^{2}\log^{2}(nT)\cdot\left\|\overline{\theta}_{n}-\theta_{n}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\mathcal{C}_{1}(1+c_{n})^{2}\log^{2}(nT)\cdot\left\|\overline{\theta}_{n}-\theta_{n}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}

Moreover, again applying (
75
),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n}),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n}),\overline{\theta}_{n})-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n}),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n}),\theta_{n}^{*})-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}-\mathcal{C}_{1}(1+\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})\|_{1})^{2}\log^{2}(nT)\cdot\|\overline{\theta}_{n}-\theta_{n}^{*}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad-\mathcal{C}_{2}(1+\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})\|_{1})\log^{2}(nT)\cdot\|\overline{\theta}_{n}-\theta_{n}^{*}\|_{2}\cdot\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta_{n}})-\big(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\big)^{T}\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{1}.

Putting both of the previous displays together, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n}),\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n}),\theta_{n}^{*})-\lambda_{n,i}\right\|_{T}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\log^{2}(nT)\cdot\left\|\overline{\theta}_{n}-\theta_{n}^{*}\right\|_{2}\Bigg(\mathcal{C}_{1}\left((1+c_{n})^{2}+(1+\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})\|_{1})^{2}\right)\|\overline{\theta}_{n}-\theta_{n}^{*}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathcal{C}_{2}(1+\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})\|_{1})\cdot\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta_{n}})-\left(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\right)^{T}\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{1}\Bigg).

Using (
76
), we conclude

\displaystyle\frac{\phi_{i,\textrm{comp}}(L;\theta_{n}^{*})^{2}}{1+|S_{i}(C_{n}^{*})|}\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*})\\
C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})-C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*})\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\log^{2}(nT)\cdot\left\|\overline{\theta}_{n}-\theta_{n}^{*}\right\|_{2}\Bigg(\mathcal{C}_{1}\left((1+c_{n})^{2}+(1+\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})\|_{1})^{2}\right)\|\overline{\theta}_{n}-\theta_{n}^{*}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathcal{C}_{2}(1+\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})\|_{1})\cdot\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta_{n}})-\left(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\right)^{T}\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{1}\Bigg).

(77)

Now, if

\left\|\overline{\theta}_{n}-\theta_{n}^{*}\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})-\left(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\right)^{T}\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{1},

we obtain from (
77
) that

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*})\\
C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})-C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\theta_{n}^{*})\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{1}\leq\frac{1+|S_{i}(C_{n}^{*})|}{\phi_{i,\textrm{comp}}(L;\theta_{n}^{*})^{2}}\log^{2}(nT)\cdot\left\|\overline{\theta}_{n}-\theta_{n}^{*}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\left(\mathcal{C}_{1}(1+c_{n})^{2}+\mathcal{C}_{1}(1+\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})\|_{1})^{2}+\mathcal{C}_{2}\left(1+\|C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})\|_{1}\right)\right).

The other case,

\left\|\overline{\theta}_{n}-\theta_{n}^{*}\right\|_{2}\geq\left\|\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{n,i}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}\\
C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}(\overline{\theta}_{n})-\left(C_{n,i\boldsymbol{\cdot}}^{*}\right)^{T}\end{pmatrix}\right\|_{1},

(78)

is stronger than what we wanted to prove.
To see this, we observe the following for any
(C,\alpha)\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{n}(i;\theta_{n}^{*},L)
.
By Assumptions (A1) and (A2) and the definition of
\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}_{n}(i;\theta_{n}^{*},L)
, we have on
\Omega_{\mathcal{N}}
that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\left\|\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{i\cdot},\alpha_{i},\theta_{n}^{*})-\Psi_{n,i}(\cdot;C_{n,i\cdot}^{*},\alpha_{n,i}^{*},\theta_{n}^{*})\right\|_{T}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T}\int_{0}^{T}\left((\alpha_{i}-\alpha_{n.i}^{*})\nu_{0}(X_{n,i}(t);\beta_{n}^{*})+\sum_{j=1}^{n}(C_{ij}-C_{n,ij}^{*})\int_{0}^{t-}g(t-r;\gamma_{n}^{*})dN_{n,j}(r)\right)^{2}dt

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{T}\int_{0}^{T}(\alpha_{i}-\alpha_{n.i}^{*})^{2}\|\overline{\nu}\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\overline{g}^{2}\log^{2}(nT)\left(\left\|C_{iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}-C_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}^{*}\right\|_{1}+\left\|(C_{iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})^{c}}-C_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})^{c}}^{*}\right\|_{1}\right)^{2}dt

\displaystyle\leq 2\Bigg((\alpha_{i}-\alpha_{n.i}^{*})^{2}\|\overline{\nu}\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle+\overline{g}^{2}\log^{2}(nT)\left(\left\|C_{iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}-C_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}^{*}\right\|_{1}+7\left\|(C_{iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}-C_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}^{*}\right\|_{1}+3L|\alpha_{i}-\alpha_{n,i}^{*}|\right)^{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\overline{k}\log^{2}(nT)\left((\alpha_{i}-\alpha_{n.i}^{*})^{2}+\left\|C_{iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}-C_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}^{*}\right\|_{1}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\overline{k}\log^{2}(nT)\left((\alpha_{i}-\alpha_{n.i}^{*})^{2}+\left\|C_{iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}-C_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}|S_{i}(C_{n}^{*})|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\overline{k}\log^{2}(nT)\left((\alpha_{i}-\alpha_{n.i}^{*})^{2}+\left\|C_{iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}-C_{n,iS_{i}(C_{n}^{*})}^{*}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)(1+|S_{i}(C_{n}^{*})|),

where
\overline{k}
is a suitably chosen constant.
We hence obtain

\displaystyle\phi_{i,\text{comp}}(L;\theta_{n}^{*})^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\overline{k}\log^{2}(nT)(1+|S_{i}(C_{n}^{*})|).

The above, together with Inequality (
78
), implies the statement that we wanted to prove on
\Omega_{\mathcal{N}}
.
The proof is complete because
\mathbb{P}(\Omega_{\mathcal{N}})\to 1
.
∎

Appendix J
Presentation of additional simulation results

J.1
More details on the simulation presented in the main text

In this section, we collect further results of the simulation study presented in Section
4.3
. Figure
6
shows a visual account of the estimation of
\alpha_{n}^{*}
. The slim-oracle model which omits the covariate shows here a strong bias.

Figure 6
:
Box plots of the estimated values for
\alpha_{n,i}
in all scenarios. The solid lines show the true values of
\alpha_{n,i}
in decreasing order. De-biasing is used together with a thresholding step.

Figure
7
shows that the in the third stage (including the thresholding step), considerably fewer edges are collected.
In the main text, we argue that this effect primarily reduces the false positives and does not so much lead to any missed detections.
This can also be seen in Figure
8
which shows the average estimates in the three models.
However, the root mean squared error (RMSE) is not so different in the three cases, cf. Figure
9

Figure 7
:
Histograms of the estimated number of edges. Solid lines show the true number of edges (13), and the dashed lines show the average of the estimated number of edges in each set-up. De-biasing is used together with a thresholding step.

Figure 8
:
Average estimates of all entries of the matrix
C_{n}
in the respective settings. The dotted line shows the true values. De-biasing is used together with a thresholding step.

Figure 9
:
Histograms of mean squared errors for the entire network. The dashed lines show the averages in the respective situation. De-biasing is used together with a thresholding step.

J.2
Larger network size

In order to illustrate the performance of our estimator in a more challenging situation, we have also considered the situation of
n=30
vertices that are observed over a time horizon of
T=16
days.
In other words, we have now more vertices and a shorter time window.
The network parameter
C_{n}^{*}
has
43
non-zero edges (out of
900
).
It is shown in the left panel of Figure
12
.
As for the other example, we consider
300
Monte Carlo replicates and choose the bandwidth parameters once via cross-validation (reusing the same values in all Monte Carlo steps).

Figures
10
and
11
show the estimators before and after the de-biasing step (including the thresholding) for
\beta_{n}^{*}
and
\gamma_{n}^{*}
.
Our general theory predicts that estimation of
\beta_{n}^{*}
and
\gamma_{n}^{*}
should work equally well as for the smaller model because
\sqrt{nT}
is in both cases roughly the same.
We see that the estimation of
\beta_{n}^{*}
indeed is very similar as for the smaller model.
For the estimation of
\gamma_{n}^{*}
, however, we see that the first stage estimator seems unstable, and very often a result close to the boundary of the optimization region is obtained.
On the positive side, we see that the de-biasing step can counteract these defect relatively well.
In addition to a reduced (but non-vanishing bias), the estimates for
\gamma_{n}^{*}
look close to a normal distribution.
Nevertheless, we suspect that the initial choice of penalty was not ideal or that the cross-validation bandwidth from one dataset cannot be directly transferred to a different dataset.

Figure 10
:
Histograms of the estimators for
\beta_{n}^{*}
before and after de-biasing (including the thresholding); the true value is shown as solid line.

Figure 11
:
Histograms of the estimators for
\gamma_{n}^{*}
before and after de-biasing (including the thresholding); the true value is shown as solid line.

When it comes to estimation of the network, we expect a lower performance of the method compared to the smaller network because the amount of data is reduced while the parameter number increases.
We see in Figure
12
on the right hand side those edges that received the highest average influence estimates in the
300
Monte Carlo repetitions.
We have restricted to the
43
largest estimates, the number of true edges.
It can be seen that many edges are detected correctly, and in particular the disjoint partitions of the true network are not linked by large estimates.
To look at this in more detail, we show in Figure
13
how often each edge was detected.
We see that about
30
of the frequently detected edges are detected more often than non-existing edges (by the 3rd stage estimator).
But, compared to the small model, there is a high-number of false positives.
This is to be expected, because the estimation task is now more difficult.
Interestingly, the frequently detected edges are not necessarily those that have the highest true influence.
Similarly as for the smaller network, we observe that the 3rd stage estimator after the de-biasing shows fewer false positives compared to the other methods.
This indicated that inclusion of covariates and de-biasing can have a benficial effect on network estimation.

Figure 12
:
True network on the left and the
43
edges that have the highest average estimate of influences (on the right).

Figure 13
:
Percentage of detections per edge. The x-axis shows the indices of the edges (in decreasing frequency of detection in the 3rd stage); the three solid lines corresponding to the models show how often the respective edge is detected. The dashed line shows the actual value of the influence of the corresponding edge (right axis).
